## `AFG-1709-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Fluorescent-Agent Inspector
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Tissue Paper Absorption, Wet Strength & Fluorescent Whitening
- **Intro:** Fluorescent-Agent Inspector — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Tissue Paper Absorption, Wet Strength & Fluorescent Whitening — Pre-Start Verification and PPE Inspection: Line speed: 300 m/min ± 5 m/min; FWA pump pressure: 0.4 MPa ± 0.02 MPa; Drying cylinder surface temperature: 110°C ± 2°C.
- **Key tolerance:** Line speed: 300 m/min ± 5 m/min; FWA pump pressure: 0.4 MPa ± 0.02 MPa; Drying cylinder surface temperature: 110°C ± 2°C.
- **Standards:** `AFS 2018`, `ISO 12625-3:2014`, `ISO 12625-8:2011`, `ISO 11475:2017`
- **Detail pages:** [EN](https://sop.go2abroad.space/en/sop/AFG-1709-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [中文](https://sop.go2abroad.space/zh/sop/AFG-1709-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [DE](https://sop.go2abroad.space/de/sop/AFG-1709-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [日本語](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ja/sop/AFG-1709-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [FR](https://sop.go2abroad.space/fr/sop/AFG-1709-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [ES](https://sop.go2abroad.space/es/sop/AFG-1709-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [PT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/pt/sop/AFG-1709-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [IT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/it/sop/AFG-1709-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [한국어](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ko/sop/AFG-1709-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [العربية](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ar/sop/AFG-1709-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [हिन्दी](https://sop.go2abroad.space/hi/sop/AFG-1709-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html)

## `AFG-1709-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-02`

- **Role:** Fluorescent-Agent Inspector
- **Scenario:** Raw Material Batch Quality Verification, NDT Inspection & Defect Failure Analysis SOP for Tissue Paper Absorption, Wet Strength & Fluorescent Whitening

## `AFG-1709-MGT-R4-MGT-SC-02`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `MGT`
- **Role:** Static-Discharge Safety Officer
- **Scenario:** 4M1E Engineering Change (ECN) Containment Approval & 8D/CAPA Root Cause Rectification SOP for Tissue Paper Slitting Dust Extraction & Combustible Lint Fire Incidents
